## 2026-10-29：Sandbox 全新上线边界

用户明确当前 Sandbox 全新上线，没有待兼容或迁移的历史 Sandbox 数据；先前将历史 Sandbox 或历史命名空间兼容、旧待派发记录 preflight 列为本次上线前置的判断不适用。租户创建时已有命名空间；初始化过程的具体命名、标签及权限绑定仍需在后续现场核验。没有历史数据不排除新请求的结果不确定，因此 Pod/PVC 的未知写入与 UID 删除屏障仍保留。

在 Resource 工作区中将当前租户 Namespace UID 加入新创建规格、摘要及持久快照，首次受理和 worker 操作时复核；已有新请求准确重放仍依据持久事实。Pod 选择 Linux/amd64 节点，与当前镜像解析的平台一致，并允许 RuntimeClass 合并其他节点选择条件。Namespace UID 检查和 Kubernetes 子资源写入不是原子操作；Namespace 替换发生在两次调用之间时，事后发现不一致只能保留 unknown，不能断言写入未发生。命名空间消失或 UID 改变时暂停、删除也可能需要可信证据和受控恢复。Gov 固定的旧 Resource 模块不能处理新增 UID 字段，须在用户安排的后续 Gov 依赖更新中同步并验证；当前未改 Gov、部署或发布。

自动启动、超时和出网是新 Sandbox 的功能选择，不能仅凭“没有历史数据”认定不需要；新请求目前尚未承接这些设置。运行中拉取凭据自动同步、Image 轮换期间的凭据切换、真实集群权限及完整生命周期仍未完成或未验证。以下状态属于先前快照。

## 2026-10-29：Sandbox 交叉审查后修复

用户说明租户创建时会初始化命名空间，Sandbox 无需通过新建 VPC 或实例触发初始化；现有 Resource 租户命名和归属标签仍须与该初始化流程核对。此轮修复 Image 精确引用查询，消除模糊结果超过 100 条时拒绝合法镜像的问题；创建前检查租户拉取凭据，已受理命令仍按原事实重放。Pod 观察校验执行命令及安全规格，未知创建仍要求匹配 mutation ID 和 UID；运行失败提供受控原因并保留终止 Pod，避免无限重建。Provider 在观察和写入前后重新检查租户命名空间归属；PV 读取超时时为归属复核保留预算。Sandbox 自身启动时数据库或迁移暂不可用会后台重试，恢复后在同一进程启用服务和 worker。新增[共享身份的 Sandbox RBAC 清单](../../deployments/sandbox/README.md)，未在集群执行。

**未完成与未验证：** 旧创建请求的自动启动、超时及出网设置尚未接入新合同；旧业务出网白名单未找到实际生效证据。新 Pod 仍固定 shell 命令、非 root 和只读根目录，与旧镜像默认命令的兼容取舍待确认；Image 目录登记不保证镜像满足这些运行条件。运行中拉取凭据没有自动刷新；混合架构调度尚未冻结架构；Kubernetes 命名空间检查与子资源写入无法原子化，历史快照也未保存 Namespace UID，旧固定命名空间的兼容须核验。租户初始化的标签与权限绑定、集群准入、真实创建及暂停恢复删除、Gov 正式 Resource 依赖、发布和部署均为 **not_verified**。以下共享入口状态属于先前快照。

## 2026-10-29：Sandbox 共享入口与配置

Resource 工作区移除 `ANI_SANDBOX_CONFIG_FILE` 装配依赖：Sandbox 共用主入口、Compute 数据库及 cluster、Resource Kubernetes 配置和租户命名空间，Compute 与 Network 的 cluster 不一致时 Sandbox 返回不可用。工作盘默认 `5Gi`、运行时默认 `sandbox-kata`，StorageClass 从唯一且满足回收要求的平台默认类读取。Image 使用本进程目录校验，当前仅接受已登记的租户或平台镜像，拉取凭据使用 Image 能力。未知创建观察到的 Pod/PVC 必须带有对应 mutation ID 与非空 UID 才能解除屏障；不符或历史缺失注解时保持 unknown。细节见[当前设计](../plans/sandbox-runtime-design-20261025.md)。

旧模板镜像是否已登记尚未核实；未登记的旧镜像当前不能直接创建，注册引用查询结果超过 100 条时会保守返回不可用。旧前端自动启动、超时、出网等功能尚未完整迁移；运行中 Pod 的拉取凭据没有后台自动刷新。目标集群默认 StorageClass、RuntimeClass、租户命名空间、Secret 和其他 Kubernetes 权限、新迁移、Gov 正式 Resource 依赖及真实 create/pause/resume/delete 均为 **not_verified**；未部署或发布。以下独立创建结果属于先前快照。

## 既有 Network 实施状态：NET-VPC-LB-02

2026-09-18 [LB 正式修复及 CI 修正](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/lb-fix-20260918/README.md)已实现：LB 关系核验前后两处审计失效均改为在原调用截止时间内重新完整采集，保留身份、时效与并发检查；无截止时间调用仍最多一次刷新，取消/超时不返回可用证明。原代码在私网/双入口均能复现剩余约 27 秒却提前 unknown/PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE；修复后的真实 PG/adapter/worker 单次调用在约 5.5 秒内 configured/fresh，Provider 对象摘要不变。Fedora UTC 环境中相关 PostgreSQL/race 27 项顶层测试、54 项子测试及 `make verify` 均 pass，包含六种真实进程恢复场景。四个隔离 PG 容器已清理，本轮没有集群写入。首次修复提交 `a7ff144` 已推送原分支，其 CI 的 Verify 与完整 PG/进程恢复均 pass；全仓 race 触发 Go 默认整包 10 分钟时限，未报告竞争或断言失败。现为全仓 integration/race 显式设置 20 分钟测试预算；产品请求超时不变。预算补正提交及后续 CI 以最终发布回执为准。此结果不扩展为真实双入口稳定窗口或所有历史退化均修复。

上一提交 `83a4346` 的两组 CI 失败也已定位：进程测试缺少必填健康检查端口，现已补齐；更新回执比较是 UTC 环境下 `time.Local` 与 `time.UTC` 的相同时刻被 reflect.DeepEqual 误判，现改为比较完整 JSON 回执，产品幂等实现未变。原始 red 与隔离字段差异留证。前序 [LB 分支诊断](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/lb-branch-20260917/README.md)保留其诊断时点事实。

Public EIP .194/.195 已由产品 API 分配成功。纯 Public LB `lb_bd6f74b7445b4c5a815763d38a995f14` 在 [r17](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/runtime-resume-r17.json) 达到 [Available/configured](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-lb-ready-r17.json)，两个 Envoy 副本及生成 Service/EIP 占用身份合法。但从 VPC 外直接请求 .194:8080 的 [24 次验收](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-lb-external-no-snat-assessment.json)全部超时，该时段[没有 Public SNAT](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-lb-no-public-snat-final.json)。[对照](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-entry-controls.json)确认同一外部客户端直连 Public 实验 Pod 成功；[被动抓包](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-entry-packet-capture.json)看到 EIP ARP 回应及 ens35 上的重复 SYN，没有 SYN-ACK，尚未定位具体 Provider 转发环节。不能将该失败归为 VLAN 未贯通，也不能用直接 Envoy Pod IP HTTP 成功替代 EIP 验收。

Public SNAT `snat_c2e679a19be64badbc78877d8323451d` 使用另一 EIP .195，[配置启用及观察](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-snat-ready.json)已完成；[真实出站连接和接收端源地址](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-snat-source-enabled.json)失败，未获得 HTTP 响应或 .195 的接收端连接证据。同期 [private VIP 12 次](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/private-vip-public-snat-enabled-assessment.json)仍通过，命中两个后端。[Public SNAT 启用、停用、重新启用、解绑的隔离验收](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-snat-lifecycle-assessment.json)已经通过：四阶段 48 次 VIP 请求、32 个内网检查通过，VPC/基础 EIP/SNAT UID 保持，Public EIP 原 UID 保留且回到 Available。额外的关闭/解绑过程采样也通过；有限采样不等于全程无间断。纯 Public LB 的[产品删除](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-lb-delete-assessment.json)已在 10:12 UTC 完成：生成资源、claim、组件与父占用全部释放，原业务 Pod UID 不变。首次等待早于 360 秒优雅退出期限，后续正常完成，没有强制清理。原 .194 的[SNAT 重绑](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-eip-reuse-assessment.json)已实际完成，EIP 原 UID 不变；.195/VIP .201 的[双入口 LB 验收](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-private-assessment.json)已实际执行：同一创建 operation 于 10:40 UTC 成功，small 两副本/生成身份正确；12 对并行入口请求全部重叠，VIP 12/12 成功、两后端各 6 次，EIP 0/12，整体双入口记 fail。重绑测试的 Public SNAT 已经再次通过产品解绑。

09:02 UTC r15 因原 ubuntu load1=8.42 受保护退出，随后 r16 同构建恢复完成原 EIP operation。09:25 UTC 在保存 [PG 备份](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/before-worker-concurrency-0924.json)后，仅将隔离实例每类 worker 并发从 2 调为已支持的 4，详见[配置对照](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/worker-concurrency-tuning.json)；r17 的二进制、数据库、租约与观察时限均不变。此后 Public LB 收敛，但不能据此认定既有观察稳定性问题已解决。基础内网两个节点的 DNS 和 DNS/Envoy HTTP [复查通过](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/base-intranet-r15-renewal.json)，[Intranet 资格](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/intranet-verification-renewal-r15.json)有效至 14:57 UTC。新增三个平台探测 Pod、两个最小 RBAC 对象仍保留，后续清理必须覆盖。

10:25 UTC [r17 因共享主机 load1=10.05 触发资源保护](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/runtime-r17-resource-stop.json)，PG/集群对象保留；之后一次重绑 SNAT 删除 RPC 在连接 socket 前失败，未受理。资源回落后经 30 秒复核，以同一构建/配置/数据库在 [r18](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/runtime-resume-r18.json) 恢复，继续原双入口 operation，不重做任何初始化或旧 VLAN 恢复。

[首次超时对照](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/audit-window-config-comparison.json)未启动：r19 被[既有配置上限](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/r19-startup-diagnostic.json)拒绝，其 [30 次未连接采样](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/private-lb-r19-stability-assessment.json)不构成产品观察结果。随后按现有上限[修正对照参数](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/audit-window-config-comparison-02.json)：audit_timeout 10→15 秒、request_timeout 20→30 秒、lease 80→120 秒；每类 4 worker、新鲜度 60 秒、资源配额和二进制不变。此前 [r18 指标](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/r18-observation-metrics-1043.json)有 10 次审计失败、成功采集最长 9.13 秒，根因仍需验证。已保留[变更前备份](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/before-audit-window-1049.json)。[r20](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/runtime-resume-r20.json) 已通过实际 RPC 读取确认启动；[固定 30 样本对照](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/private-lb-r20-stability-assessment.json)仍 fail：11 configured、17 ProviderNotReady、2 BackendIdentityMismatch。该窗口没有审计失败或 stale，放宽时限未解决稳定性。随后已[暂停并备份](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/before-observation-debug-1125.json)，仅在本 run 准备临时日志。诊断构建 `20260915T112356Z-8a8f27c9` 在 r22 实际运行，[30 次采样](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/observation-debug-assessment.json)27 configured、3 unknown，仍 fail；已捕获 Snat 审计快照失效、应用事实暂未知、LB 依赖检查失败的路径，但没有证明所有历史退化原因相同。临时源码和二进制[已经撤回](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/observation-debug-restored.json)，[r23](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/runtime-resume-r23.json) 恢复原合格构建并通过[实际 RPC](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/qualified-restored-r23-read.json)，没有把诊断当成修复。

用户授权的[新集群与恢复输入](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/resume-20260915.md)保持：172.16.101.10–12，来宾 untagged，上游逻辑 VLAN 102，172.16.102.0/24，网关 .1。[ani-01 网关往返与临时配置清理](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/l2-diagnostic-20260915/untagged-followup.md)已保留；测试 VM 172.16.102.30 已由用户关机，不再重测。管理员二层排障接口仅有[建议](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/l2-diagnostic-20260915/interface-proposal.md)，没有扩大本批实现范围。

[跨 namespace 外来 EIP Service 保护](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/cross-namespace-service-assessment.json)已通过：Tenant B 的独立 Service 引用 Tenant A 双入口 LB 的 EIP，LB 报告归属冲突；外来 Service 和合法生成资源 UID/spec 均保留。owner 按 UID 删除外来对象后正常 worker 恢复，新增两项 RBAC 已删除。此项不代替已有生成 Service 的 UID 替换场景。

按用户最新要求，三项 Public 失败停止继续排查并交接：[Public 转发排查记录](records/NET-VPC-LB-02/device-registration-20260915/public-forwarding-followup.md)集中保存地址、版本、成功对照、抓包、OVN 只读结果及复查命令。网络插件或外部网络配置只是待排查方向，根因未确认；原 fail 保留，整体 Goal 仍未完成。其余范围内验收、观察问题和清理继续独立记账。

## 历史分支提交与当前边界

NET-02–04 后续发布记录属于历史输入：Network 固定提交已发布；ANI 固定提交仅保留本地，用户最新决定暂缓推送。NET-05 原 Goal 的验收阶段只允许隔离验收与远端临时验证提交；验收结束后用户明确授权发布 Network 至远端 main，并再次确认 ANI 继续保留本地。KC-KIND 后续记录作为单独环境输入，不混入固定业务源码。

## 历史下一步安排

NET-05A 按用户调整后的范围结束并停止；Goal 结束后的独立提交推送按本页末尾授权执行。1,000/2,000 容量未完成，后续在资源条件具备且独立恢复该工作后验证；不自动启动 NET-06、配额或发布。以下 NET-05 发布说明为历史安排。

NET-05 验收完成，按用户后续授权发布 Network main，ANI 成果继续保持本地未提交状态。NET-06、NET-AUTH、Console、生产发布/升级和整体切换等待各自独立授权；不自动继续。

## Network main 发布授权

2026-09-10 用户在验收与清理结束后明确要求提交远端 main，并确认仅发布 Network，ANI 继续保留本地。发布从 `87e91de53aff9158a0525950c59de8a338f01ccc` 建立独立 worktree；该提交是 NET-02–04 的等价 squash，其 tree 与固定 `5d4a534` 完全相同。发布范围为已验收 NET-05 成果和本段授权记录，原验收工作树及封存证据保留。提交前校验完整发布树的 verify/audit/SBOM；远端实际提交及 CI 状态以 GitHub main 与对应提交 checks 为准，不将本地门禁冒称云端 CI。

发布快照按已校验清单显式暂存文件，保留本次明确纳入 Git 的脱敏日志，避免默认 `*.log` 忽略规则使远端验证/SBOM 输入少于实际发布树。该修复只影响源码快照传递，不改变业务运行行为。

## NET-05A 后续提交推送授权

2026-09-10，用户在 NET-05A Goal 关闭后明确要求提交并推送 Network service。目标为 `origin/codex/net-05a`，包含已验收的实现、迁移、测试运行器、文档和脱敏证据；原 Goal 完成记录中的“未提交”描述保留为当时事实。本次授权不包含其他仓库、NET-06、配额、大规模容量补测、registry 镜像发布或整体切换。

提交前以完整待提交文件清单在 ubuntu 单条受限流水线运行 `make verify`、`make audit`，为包含脱敏证据的实际提交内容重新生成 SBOM。验收阶段排除原始证据的源快照与本次提交内容分别标识；精确远端提交和对应 CI 结果以远端分支与该 SHA 的 checks 为准，不用临时验证提交冒充发布版本。

## VPC SNAT 后续分支提交推送授权

2026-09-11 用户明确选择完成本仓交付收尾与提交推送，目标为 `origin/codex/vpc-snat-implementation`。[发布记录](records/VPC-SNAT-PUBLICATION-20260911/README.md)保留完整暂存树、ubuntu 门禁、历史证据的精确属性处理和最终 SBOM 流程。首轮 `make verify`、6 项 tenant-mutations、漏洞/密钥/SBOM/notice 门禁 `pass`；原全量 PG/race 对应的 140 项运行源码仍保持。当前交付分支的真实提交和 exact-SHA CI 以 Git/托管平台为准，不将临时验证提交冒充发布版本。

本次源码交付不解除 kc 外部阻塞，不宣布原生 Overlay 出网通过。Underlay 物理、ANI Gateway/IAM、合并 main、PR、镜像发布和部署继续保持各自边界。

## GPU container instance MVP v1.5

2026-10-13 用户启动持续 Goal；后续明确本轮只负责后端，前端由专人接手。用户随后授权等待 GPU 期间适配实际调度器、准备真实隔离后端环境并完成 CPU 基础设施验证；这些独立工作及实际 Image 接线已推进。Goal **blocked / 未完成**：恢复后的原用户轮及后续两轮中，同一实际 GPU 前提持续缺失；本轮 Fedora 只读复核确认三节点 GPU allocatable 空、设备插件无、scheduler 没有 deviceshare/VGPUEnable，四个任务服务 inactive/dead、ExecMainStatus=0，没有可等待的运行验收任务。当前必须依赖平台条件变化才能继续真实 GPU 产品主链；不安装缺失组件，不用 CPU/替身结果替代共享/整卡验收。原 MVP-01～04 后端目标保持。

主链：Gov 当前授权 → 原公开请求幂等 → Image/preset/Namespace 冻结 → Acc plan → Gov 原子占额/持久投递 → Res 持久 ACK → Notebook GPU → SG 终端/工作目录 → 原账启停 → 删除计算及专属盘 → Gov 原账归还 → Acc ENDED。

### 当前业务位置

Resource 已实现独立实例持久仓储、ACK、worker、Notebook/PVC 适配、Image 窄运行用例、原 charge 退额 outbox 和 SG 目标复核。真实 PG + 外部 Kubernetes/Gov 替身覆盖多次启停、原冻结配置不变、PVC 清理失败后 GPU 已归还但删除未完成、未知 CREATE 保持 blocked、DELETE 先到和租户拒绝。**这还不是 Gov 产品 API → GPU → 终端的闭环证据。**

Gov 公开 HTTP 已通过真实 JWT/token cache、套餐闸门、Casbin 和当前角色快照验证；创建/重放、列表/详情/操作/规格查询、Stop/Start 及最终 Delete 经真实 mTLS 接到 Resource 子进程与独立受限 PG，CREATE/DELETE 均取得持久 ACK。此切片中的 Acc RPC 和 Image/环境可用事实受控，没有运行 Notebook，也没有制造计算清理或退额：最终删除接单后原账仍占 6144 MiB。源与记录见 [本次后端证据](records/INSTANCE-MVP-V15/README.md)。

四个服务的目标构建、必要 Proto/Ent/sqlc 生成已通过 Fedora 工作区验证。Acc 固定 Resource ref/投影、独立 owner 公钥拒绝旧 Inference 密钥；SG UID guard、Redis 绑定重启恢复与受影响旧 gRPC 路径已通过定向检查。Resource 生产退额 outbox 已通过回执丢失和重启后原事件重发。

后续同一 HTTP 切片启用真实 Resource worker/退额 outbox、Gov 退额 mTLS listener 及 Acc 服务/PG：Acc 目录通过管理 RPC 准备，公开创建通过真实 Acc RPC 冻结 plan；运行观察、Stop/Start、计算/盘清理决策、原事件退额、Gov 原账归零和 Acc ENDED 查询均通过（45 秒，exit 0）。此阶段仅 Kubernetes provider 与 Image/环境事实受控；没有真实 GPU/Notebook/物理盘，也没有 SG 终端或文件验收。没有手改原账或注入完成通知。

进一步在同一切片接入真实 SG 服务：Gov 唯一 URI mTLS 签发、SG 唯一 URI mTLS 查询 Resource 的窄终端 validator、独占真实 Redis、原 WebSocket/Connected Session/ExecAdapter 均使用生产代码。45 秒组合用例通过（exit 0）：shell 写入/读回任务文件、Stop/Start 后相同目录读回；停止后旧票据和旧版本票据在重启后均无法打开 exec；随后删除/退额/Acc ENDED 仍通过。Kubernetes 列表/remotecommand 使用显式边界替身，文件为 task-local 外部 exec 工作目录，**不是实际 PVC 文件或 GPU 容器验收**。当前共享环境仍未创建实例。

接线发现 Acc schema 2 的 usages/claims/release_proofs 只允许 Inference。新增独立 `0003_resource_instance_owner.sql`，runtime 明确要求 schema 3，不改历史迁移、不自动迁移。新迁移在独占 PG 实际执行，并通过原 Inference/Resource 的 Binding 先到、投影关联、ENDED 不等于分配消失和确定释放用例；旧投影并发/恢复定向回归通过。正式启动与回退必须使用匹配数据库版本/恢复点；尚未更新共享 Acc 服务。

## GPU absolute memory 2026-10-18

Implementation in progress under the fixed external task Goal. The new owner
command accepts one context and one managed spec, with a single GPU execution
snapshot and display metadata. Quota items remain in Governance; Resource's
release outbox sends only event/create-operation/resource/reason and checks all
matching receipt references before acknowledgement. RuntimeClass comes from
ACC execution only. The Pod signature is `acc-owner-pod-v2` and binds the actual
Pod UID to `execution_digest`.

The explicit `004_gpu_execution.sql` cutover rejects active GPU state or an
unacknowledged release outbox. It retains original snapshots in an audit table,
keeps terminal operation references and disables existing GPU Presets for explicit
replacement. Runtime never decodes archived GPU formats. Fedora `make generate`, the real PostgreSQL instance lifecycle suite and the
repository-required `make verify` passed with formal ACC
`93a539326e409ae9437b95faebd050624273fd22` and quota API
`1c605c793b88e460a84e64a88cbfa91c0a801afa` dependencies (`GOWORK=off`, exact
module checksums and ACC Origin.Hash checked). The first verification run retained
an unrelated Unix-socket path-length failure under the long task TMPDIR; the same
test and complete required gate passed with an exclusive short TMPDIR, without
changing its code. Explicit migration tests rejected active GPU/unacknowledged
outbox state and preserved terminal audit and ACK records after closure.

Owner code is delivered through existing Resource PR #8 without merge. New GPU
deployment, CUDA, physical release, capacity restoration and original-ledger
refund remain `NOT_RUN` for this owner-code receipt; the task's final integrated
receipt must establish them separately. Raw logs and exact module receipts stay
outside source under the dedicated `gpu-catalog-absolute-memory-20261018/res`
task directory.

## Instance configuration and network 2026-10-19

Goal `01a15337-2e0a-7083-8b97-043204896c8d` implements the [configuration/network contract](../specs/instance-configuration-network.md) and [IC plan](../plans/instance-configuration-network.md). This is the only current execution ledger. **All mandatory backend behavior and deployed-business-version checks have passed. The actual CPU/CSI/VPC/GPU lifecycle, main integration, final test-environment upgrade and independent cleanup are complete.** Browser integration is independently `not_verified` and belongs to the frontend owner.

### Actual product results

Authenticated tenant Gov CREATE → current permissions/plan → mTLS Resource → restricted PostgreSQL → real Notebook/Pod/CSI → formal SG WebSocket terminal passed. CPU instance `5d54dd92-d96f-4b75-bcdb-87da5436e0c8` used two borrowed PVCs, custom paths/workdir/metadata and ordered literal env. Reads/writes, read-only rejection, two Stop/Start rounds, closed-new-write replay, normal reader restarts, dedicated PVC/PV reclamation and post-delete borrowed-file retention passed.

CPU VPC instance `d976e3a7-082c-4d9d-9d7a-890141897b09` selected its actual subnet IP and communicated with the controlled same-VPC peer through SG. The original accepted instance recovered without replacing its CREATE. Full Stop revoked current address eligibility; final deletion released snapshot 6 and all saved Pod/VNic/VNicIP/Nic identities. A sibling Notebook Service had continuously invalidated address proofs; the implementation now limits these proof facts to Pod/VNic/VNicIP/EIP while preserving related-object, source-continuity and overflow invalidation. Isolated product services, namespaces, fixtures and image resources were subsequently normally cleaned; the separate CI PG and its sole Goal-owned volume were also normally removed after the final Gov gates, with no residual test resources.

The shared tenant-a GPU instance `7919d13c-85c6-4467-98ff-403f81bf6444` reused the existing ACC supply group and T4. Three original borrowed PVC/PV identities, custom configuration, read-only denial, two Stop/Start rounds, automatic Pod rebuild, maintenance replay/rejection, normal Gov/Res restarts and actual same-VPC peer traffic passed. The original Multi-Attach event, exact Pod/PV/VolumeAttachment identities and public diagnostics were captured; normal UID deletion of its Goal-owned holder allowed ordinary CSI detach and recovery of the same accepted product Pod. CUDA dense inference ran through product SG before and after recovery: maximum absolute error `8.715166881501091e-7`, accuracy `0.9666666666666667`, original T4 UUID unchanged.

GPU product DELETE at version 5 naturally reached desired/observed `deleted`, version 6, with **all five cleanup flags true**. Independent read-only checks confirmed four original GPU Pod UIDs absent, four ACC physical-release proofs, the Res physical seal and acknowledged outbox, exact original Gov charges refunded and zero T4 memory/process use. Network snapshot 13 is released, with zero eligible addresses and all original current/historical network identities absent. A separate restricted read-only inspector then succeeded: all three original borrowed PVC/PV UIDs, sentinel files, product marker and CUDA result survived product deletion; the dedicated workspace PVC/PV were absent. The inspector and peer were normally UID-deleted. Shared Subnet `subnet_255570941ffd4ba98c61f98b17b1d0f0` and VPC `vpc_b6a63e5f766242ddbe89d3c537cbdcb8` were normally product-deleted and their original provider CRs are absent. After this retention proof, separate normal UID/RV-guarded fixture cleanup deleted the three Goal PVCs; CSI reclaimed their original PVs. The dedicated policy/binding and Goal fixture SA were then normally removed. The final receipt has no residual test objects and confirms the original shared namespace, workload SA and installed NIC permission UIDs retained.

Final Network release originally timed out for 600 seconds. Actual bound Resource-SA requests proved the cause: `nics list` in `kcn-system` returned 403. This closed-release check is outside the 14 full-observer LIST/WATCH sources. A namespace Role granting only `networking.kubercloud.com/nics: list`, bound only to the existing Resource SA, changed the actual request to 200; the original worker completed without a restart, repeated DELETE, source patch or state update. The installed Role/Binding `kcn-system/icn-01a15337-res-nic-list` are retained deployment dependencies. Existing RBAC objects remain unchanged.

### Accepted scope revisions and deployment

The user explicitly authorized upgrading the existing shared test Gov/Res, using existing tenant-a/ACC/T4, retiring old instances and terminal records, and deleting old instance-configuration compatibility. Both repositories now accept only server-frozen configuration contract 2; no default/0/1 reader, legacy digest projection or recovery fallback remains. Legitimate new-request defaults are resolved once. The GPU Execution protocol, original charges, stable refund event and physical fence retain their existing contract. Notebook Controller itself is unchanged; Res independently verifies its owner chain and recognizes only known served v1/v1beta1 names for the same Notebook UID.

Before cutover, four terminal tenant-a and twelve historical GPU-test instances had original compute/workspace identities absent, acknowledged refunds, zero physical accounts and no pending commands. Precise backed-up record cleanup then committed. Tenant-a's old network resources were normally product-deleted first. Accounts/versions, unrelated API/permission/role/plan rows, tenant identities, namespaces and user PVCs were preserved. Explicit migrations reached Network 15, Instance 6 and Gov `20261019090000`; historical migration records/checksums remain unchanged.

Installed Res image is `172.16.101.10:30003/icn-01a15337-release/resource@sha256:e32747c9006f23f8951c5d29ca056a7670980675f179225881a59f348301464d`, binary SHA256 `ad11b77ab115912ae6b50aa6a183a8a898d693d2e00fb288383172787288a287`. Existing identities, mounted keys/CA and shared ACC/SG/CNI/CSI/Notebook/scheduler were preserved. Res CPU alone changed from a half-core limit to request/limit `500m/2` after actual node headroom verification; memory remained unchanged. A subsequent 60-second window had 7/7 readiness successes and no restarts. Earlier readiness/terminal/network-wait failures remain evidence; their individual causes are not invented. Observation helpers and temporary forwards exited normally.

The final shared test environment is **open and available**. Gov image is `172.16.101.10:30003/icn-01a15337-release/governance@sha256:22085d583724b4787b609584999c4b1084f4bdbd2469c572a09b7dc74f6f91d8`; final ready Gov Pod UID is `1b8e7d95-205a-4fce-b78c-1e3e19c91fcf`, Res Pod UID `0ffa909c-50f2-4c1e-aa11-9f92b093ab91`. Both have zero restarts; Gov CREATE and Res network writes are enabled after all readers are ready. Normal tenant-a login, identity, empty mountable-fixture catalog and the deleted instance's frozen configuration all return 200; current contract, workdir, original ordered env and three frozen mounts read back correctly. New admin API dry-run/sync and metrics APIs 330/331 linked to existing `instance:read` permission 403 passed; original API identities/status, links, permissions, role and plan rows remained unchanged.
